# Agents · M · part 13

963 entries, minimalism-auditor to ml-modeler. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **minimalism-auditor**: Advisory-only minimalism review producing RiskFindings (never verdicts). Invoke for clean-context review of abstraction creep, dead layers, premature generality. ([gretjia/turingos.app](https://github.com/gretjia/turingos.app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/minimalism-auditor.md))
- **minimalist**: Reviews a system/software design as The Minimalist — blunt, impatient with unnecessary abstraction, obsessed with simplicity and "good taste" in data structures over clever code. Use when you want a no-BS gut check on whether a design is over-engineered, whether it will actually be maintainable by ordinary humans, and where it's needlessly complex. Highlights real engineering strengths too, without ceremony. ([vadeg/engineering-skills](https://github.com/vadeg/engineering-skills/blob/HEAD/commitee-of-concerns/agents/minimalist.md) · ★8)
- **minimalist**: You are a code simplifier. Your job is to make code as simple as possible, but no simpler. ([armsteadj1/claw-use](https://github.com/armsteadj1/claw-use/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/minimalist.md))

- **minimalist-refactor**: Use this agent when you want to simplify and consolidate code, eliminate dead code, unify fragmented methods, improve naming consistency, or refactor for better separation of concerns. - **minimalist-reviewer**: Dedicated Minimal Change Governance reviewer persona for Tribunal-Kit. Mission: Determine whether the proposal solves the requested problem with the smallest correct, maintainable change. ([Harmitx7/tribunal-kit](https://github.com/Harmitx7/tribunal-kit/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/minimalist-reviewer.md) · ★7)

- **mirai-gikai-checker**: みらい議会＠新宿区の機械的チェック専用（Haiku）。正確なコマンド・期待出力・合否基準が渡された作業だけに使う。例：台帳の rebuild_*.sh 実行と cmp、Tier B/C テスト（pnpm --filter @mirai-gikai/seed test）の件数報告、pdftotext 抽出、禁止トークンの grep、台帳CSVのBOM/CRLF確認、変種数・台帳行数の集計、gh pr checks の状態取得、タイムスタンプ付きバックアップ、knowledge/log.md への追記。判断を含む作業（重複かどうか、意図に合うか、台帳行が supported か、会派賛否の読み取り、audit.py の DATE-* 指摘の真偽、公開可否）には使わない。それらは証拠集めまでにとどめ NEEDS JUDGMENT で返す。作業ファイルの編集・本番操作には使わない。 ([OchiK/miraigikai-Shinjuku](https://github.com/OchiK/miraigikai-Shinjuku/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mirai-gikai-checker.md))

- **mistakes**: &gt; Scope: mistakes that apply to **every** component. Each component's own &gt; `mistakes.md` (next to its `AGENTS.md`) covers the component-specific ones; the &gt; ones below are universal. The rules they break: [`conventions.md`](conventions.md), &gt; [`variants.md`](variants.md), [`color.md`](color.md), [`theming.md`](theming.md). ([magma-design-system/magma](https://github.com/magma-design-system/magma/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/mistakes.md))
- **mistakes-that-must-not-repeat**: &gt; **Companion to [`../you/what-frustrates-you.md`](../you/what-frustrates-you.md).** &gt; That page asks what annoys you. This one asks the narrower, more actionable &gt; question: which specific mistakes should the estate make **structurally &gt; impossible**, and what should happen when one is about to occur. ([menno420/fleet-manager](https://github.com/menno420/fleet-manager/blob/HEAD/owner/intent-workbooks/agents/mistakes-that-must-not-repeat.md))
- **Mister Auto image quality**: Audit Mister Auto imagery and prepare faithful image improvements with Adobe Firefly. Requires verified API access for automated Firefly operations; works as an audit and briefing assistant without it. ([oardietadb/mister-auto-final-demo](https://github.com/oardietadb/mister-auto-final-demo/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/image-quality.agent.md))
- **mistral**: MUST BE USED when the user names Mistral Small — "ask mistral", "ask agent mistral", "use mistral", "mistral small", "what would mistral say", "mistral's take". Not for multi-file implementation — use coder instead. Not for a multi-model panel — use advisors instead. ([whichguy/c-thru](https://github.com/whichguy/c-thru/blob/HEAD/agents/mistral.md))
- **mistral**: Describe what this custom agent does and when to use it. ([xgrandjean/serveurIA_XSpro](https://github.com/xgrandjean/serveurIA_XSpro/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/mistral.agent.md))

- **mistral-small3-2-24b**: MUST BE USED only when the user types the exact agent id "mistral-small3-2-24b", character for character. Use for "ask mistral-small3-2-24b", "use mistral-small3-2-24b". Not for plain family-name asks — use the primary named agent instead. Not for multi-file implementation — use coder instead. ([whichguy/c-thru](https://github.com/whichguy/c-thru/blob/HEAD/agents/mistral-small3-2-24b.md))

- **mistrz-ciesielski**: Mistrz ciesielski i stolarski. Recenzuje konstrukcje z drewna, które drewniak generuje (szkielet, dach, podłoga, skrzynka), oraz zmiany w kodzie generatorów i ich specach — szuka wad konstrukcyjnych, złych połączeń, brakujących elementów i błędów w rozkroju. Używaj przed archiwizacją każdej zmiany, która dotyka geometrii albo listy materiałów, i gdy trzeba ocenić „czy to się utrzyma”. ([dsoosh/drewniak](https://github.com/dsoosh/drewniak/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mistrz-ciesielski.md) · 2 copies)
- **mistrz-rzemieslnik**: Mistrz rzemieślnik i projektant mebli. Pilnuje lekko nowoczesnego sznytu opartego na tradycji, bez frezowanych profili i ornamentów. Używaj przed archiwizacją każdej zmiany, która zmienia wygląd konstrukcji, i gdy trzeba ocenić „czy to dobrze wygląda”. ([dsoosh/drewniak](https://github.com/dsoosh/drewniak/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mistrz-rzemieslnik.md) · 2 copies)

- **ml-model-expert**: Use for model-layer work — loading weights, GPU memory budgeting, batching strategy, quantization (bitsandbytes/AWQ/GGUF/INT8), tokenizer/preprocessor concurrency, warmup, framework choice. Invoke whenever the request touches `app/models/` or asks "which framework / quantization / batch size". ([ThanThoai/my-claude-setup](https://github.com/ThanThoai/my-claude-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ml-model-expert.md))
- **ml-model-implementer**: Use this agent when you need to implement machine learning model code based on specifications from ML model design agents. This includes translating model architecture designs into working code, implementing training loops, loss functions, data loaders, and model components while preserving the existing repository structure. Also use this agent when test code needs to be written for ML implementations.
